data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"deepeval test run test_chatbot.py\"\u003e\u003cpre class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003edeepeval test run test_chatbot.py\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCongratulations! Your test case should have passed ✅\u003c/strong\u003e Let's break down what happened.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe variable \u003ccode\u003einput\u003c/code\u003e mimics a user input, and \u003ccode\u003eactual_output\u003c/code\u003e is a placeholder for what your application's supposed to output based on this input.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe variable \u003ccode\u003eexpected_output\u003c/code\u003e represents the ideal answer for a given \u003ccode\u003einput\u003c/code\u003e, and \u003ca href=\"https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-llm-evals\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eGEval\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e is a research-backed metric provided by \u003ccode\u003edeepeval\u003c/code\u003e for you to evaluate your LLM outputs on any custom with human-like accuracy.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIn this example, the metric \u003ccode\u003ecriteria\u003c/code\u003e is correctness of the \u003ccode\u003eactual_output\u003c/code\u003e based on the provided \u003ccode\u003eexpected_output\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAll metric scores range from 0 - 1, which the \u003ccode\u003ethreshold=0.5\u003c/code\u003e threshold ultimately determines if your test has passed or not.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://deepeval.com/docs/getting-started?utm_source=GitHub\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eRead our documentation\u003c/a\u003e for more information!\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cbr\u003e\n\u003cdiv \u003ccode\u003eevals_iterator()\u003c/code\u003e to run the same dataset through your app, whether you instrument it manually or through one of DeepEval's framework integrations.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eHere's an example of manual instrumentation:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"from deepeval.tracing import observe, update_current_span\nfrom deepeval.test_case import LLMTestCase\nfrom deepeval.metrics import TaskCompletionMetric\n\n@observe()\ndef inner_component(input: str):\n    output = \u0026quot;result\u0026quot;\n    update_current_span(test_case=LLMTestCase(input=input, actual_output=output))\n    return output\n\n@observe()\ndef app(input: str):\n    return inner_component(input)\n\n# This metric will be run on your trace end to end.\nfor golden in dataset.evals_iterator(metrics=[TaskCompletionMetric()]):\n    app(golden.input)\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003efrom\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan It is similar to Pytest but specialized for unit testing LLM apps. DeepEval incorporates the latest research to run evals via metrics such as G-Eval, task completion, answer relevancy, hallucination, etc., which uses LLM-as-a-judge and other NLP models that run **locally on your machine**.","","Whether you're building AI agents, RAG pipelines, or chatbots, implemented via LangChain or OpenAI, DeepEval has you covered. With it, you can easily determine the optimal models, prompts, and architecture to improve your AI quality, prevent prompt drifting, or even transition from OpenAI to Claude with confidence.","","\u003e [!IMPORTANT]","\u003e Need a place for your DeepEval testing data to live 🏡❤️? [Sign up to Confident AI](https://www.confident-ai.com?utm_source=deepeval\u0026utm_medium=github\u0026utm_content=signup_callout) to compare iterations of your LLM app, generate \u0026 share testing reports, and more.","\u003e","\u003e ![Demo GIF](assets/demo.gif)","","\u003e Want to talk LLM evaluation, need help picking metrics, or just to say hi? [Come join our discord.](https://discord.com/invite/3SEyvpgu2f)","","\u003cbr /\u003e","","# 🔥 Metrics and Features","","- 📐 Large variety of ready-to-use LLM eval metrics (all with explanations) powered by **ANY** LLM of your choice, statistical methods, or NLP models that run **locally on your machine** covering all use cases:","","  - **Custom, All-Purpose Metrics:**","","    - [G-Eval](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-llm-evals) — a research-backed LLM-as-a-judge metric for evaluating on any custom criteria with human-like accuracy","    - [DAG](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-dag) — DeepEval's graph-based deterministic LLM-as-a-judge metric builder","","  - \u003cdetails\u003e","    \u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eAgentic Metrics\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e","","    - [Task Completion](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-task-completion) — evaluate whether an agent accomplished its goal","    - [Tool Correctness](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-tool-correctness) — check if the right tools were called with the right arguments","    - [Goal Accuracy](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-goal-accuracy) — measure how accurately the agent achieved the intended goal","    - [Step Efficiency](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-step-efficiency) — evaluate whether the agent took unnecessary steps","    - [Plan Adherence](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-plan-adherence) — check if the agent followed the expected plan","    - [Plan Quality](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-plan-quality) — evaluate the quality of the agent's plan","    - [Tool Use](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-tool-use) — measure quality of tool usage","    - [Argument Correctness](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-argument-correctness) — validate tool call arguments","","    \u003c/details\u003e","","  - \u003cdetails\u003e","    \u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eRAG Metrics\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e","","    - [Answer Relevancy](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-answer-relevancy) — measure how relevant the RAG pipeline's output is to the input","    - [Faithfulness](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-faithfulness) — evaluate whether the RAG pipeline's output factually aligns with the retrieval context","    - [Contextual Recall](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-contextual-recall) — measure how well the RAG pipeline's retrieval context aligns with the expected output","    - [Contextual Precision](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-contextual-precision) — evaluate whether relevant nodes in the RAG pipeline's retrieval context are ranked higher","    - [Contextual Relevancy](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-contextual-relevancy) — measure the overall relevance of the RAG pipeline's retrieval context to the input","    - [RAGAS](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-ragas) — average of answer relevancy, faithfulness, contextual precision, and contextual recall","","    \u003c/details\u003e","","  - \u003cdetails\u003e","    \u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eMulti-Turn Metrics\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e","","    - [Knowledge Retention](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-knowledge-retention) — evaluate whether the chatbot retains factual information throughout a conversation","    - [Conversation Completeness](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-conversation-completeness) — measure whether the chatbot satisfies user needs throughout a conversation","    - [Turn Relevancy](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-turn-relevancy) — evaluate whether the chatbot generates consistently relevant responses throughout a conversation","    - [Turn Faithfulness](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-turn-faithfulness) — check if the chatbot's responses are factually grounded in retrieval context across turns","    - [Role Adherence](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-role-adherence) — evaluate whether the chatbot adheres to its assigned role throughout a conversation","","    \u003c/details\u003e","","  - \u003cdetails\u003e","    \u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eMCP Metrics\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e","","    - [MCP Task Completion](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-mcp-task-completion) — evaluate how effectively an MCP-based agent accomplishes a task","    - [MCP Use](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-mcp-use) — measure how effectively an agent uses its available MCP servers","    - [Multi-Turn MCP Use](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-multi-turn-mcp-use) — evaluate MCP server usage across conversation turns","","    \u003c/details\u003e","","  - \u003cdetails\u003e","    \u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eMultimodal Metrics\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e","","    - [Text to Image](https://deepeval.com/docs/multimodal-metrics-text-to-image) — evaluate image generation quality based on semantic consistency and perceptual quality","    - [Image Editing](https://deepeval.com/docs/multimodal-metrics-image-editing) — evaluate image editing quality based on semantic consistency and perceptual quality","    - [Image Coherence](https://deepeval.com/docs/multimodal-metrics-image-coherence) — measure how well images align with their accompanying text","    - [Image Helpfulness](https://deepeval.com/docs/multimodal-metrics-image-helpfulness) — evaluate how effectively images contribute to user comprehension of the text","    - [Image Reference](https://deepeval.com/docs/multimodal-metrics-image-reference) — evaluate how accurately images are referred to or explained by accompanying text","","    \u003c/details\u003e","","  - \u003cdetails\u003e","    \u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eOther Metrics\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e","","    - [Hallucination](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-hallucination) — check whether the LLM generates factually correct information against provided context","    - [Summarization](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-summarization) — evaluate whether summaries are factually correct and include necessary details","    - [Bias](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-bias) — detect gender, racial, or political bias in LLM outputs","    - [Toxicity](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-toxicity) — evaluate toxicity in LLM outputs","    - [JSON Correctness](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-json-correctness) — check whether the output matches an expected JSON schema","    - [Prompt Alignment](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-prompt-alignment) — measure whether the output aligns with instructions in the prompt template","","    \u003c/details\u003e","","- 🎯 Supports both end-to-end and component-level LLM evaluation.","- 🧩 Build your own custom metrics that are automatically integrated with DeepEval's ecosystem.","- 🔮 Generate both single and multi-turn synthetic datasets for evaluation.","- 🔗 Integrates seamlessly with **ANY** CI/CD environment.","- 🧬 Optimize prompts automatically based on evaluation results.","- 🏆 Easily benchmark **ANY** LLM on popular LLM benchmarks in [under 10 lines of code.](https://deepeval.com/docs/benchmarks-introduction?utm_source=GitHub), including MMLU, HellaSwag, DROP, BIG-Bench Hard, TruthfulQA, HumanEval, GSM8K.","","\u003cbr /\u003e","","# 🔌 Integrations","","DeepEval plugs into any LLM framework — OpenAI Agents, LangChain, CrewAI, and more. To scale evals across your team — or let anyone run them without writing code — **Confident AI** gives you a native platform integration.","","## Frameworks","","- [OpenAI](https://www.deepeval.com/integrations/frameworks/openai?utm_source=GitHub) — evaluate and trace OpenAI applications via a client wrapper","- [OpenAI Agents](https://www.deepeval.com/integrations/frameworks/openai-agents?utm_source=GitHub) — evaluate OpenAI Agents end-to-end in under a minute","- [LangChain](https://www.deepeval.com/integrations/frameworks/langchain?utm_source=GitHub) — evaluate LangChain applications with a callback handler","- [LangGraph](https://www.deepeval.com/integrations/frameworks/langgraph?utm_source=GitHub) — evaluate LangGraph agents with a callback handler","- [Pydantic AI](https://www.deepeval.com/integrations/frameworks/pydanticai?utm_source=GitHub) — evaluate Pydantic AI agents with type-safe validation","- [CrewAI](https://www.deepeval.com/integrations/frameworks/crewai?utm_source=GitHub) — evaluate CrewAI multi-agent systems","- [Anthropic](https://www.deepeval.com/integrations/frameworks/anthropic?utm_source=GitHub) — evaluate and trace Claude applications via a client wrapper","- [AWS AgentCore](https://www.deepeval.com/integrations/frameworks/agentcore?utm_source=GitHub) — evaluate agents deployed on Amazon AgentCore","- [LlamaIndex](https://www.deepeval.com/integrations/frameworks/llamaindex?utm_source=GitHub) — evaluate RAG applications built with LlamaIndex","","## ☁️ Platform + Ecosystem","","[Confident AI](https://www.confident-ai.com?utm_source=deepeval\u0026utm_medium=github\u0026utm_content=platform_section) is an all-in-one platform that integrates natively with DeepEval.","","- Manage datasets, trace LLM applications, run evaluations, and monitor responses in production — all from one platform.","- Don't need a UI? Confident AI can also be your data persistent layer - run evals, pull datasets, and inspect traces straight from claude code, cursor, via Confident AI's [MCP server](https://github.com/confident-ai/confident-mcp-server).","","\u003cp align=\"center\"\u003e","  \u003cimg src=\"assets/confident-mcp-architecture.png\" alt=\"Confident AI MCP Architecture\" width=\"500\"\u003e","\u003c/p\u003e","","\u003cbr /\u003e","","# 🤖 Vibe-Coder QuickStart","","Want your coding agent to add evals and fix failures for you? Install the DeepEval skill, point it at your agent, RAG pipeline, or chatbot, and ask it to generate a dataset, write the eval suite, run `deepeval test run`, and iterate on the failing metrics.","","[Start with the 5-minute vibe-coder guide](https://deepeval.com/docs/vibe-coder-quickstart?utm_source=GitHub).","","\u003cbr /\u003e","","# 🚀 Human QuickStart","","Let's pretend your LLM application is a RAG based customer support chatbot; here's how DeepEval can help test what you've built.","","## Installation","","Deepeval works with **Python\u003e=3.9+**.","","```","pip install -U deepeval","```","","## Create an account (highly recommended)","","Using the `deepeval` platform will allow you to generate sharable testing reports on the cloud. It is free, takes no additional code to setup, and we highly recommend giving it a try.","","To login, run:","","```","deepeval login","```","","Follow the instructions in the CLI to create an account, copy your API key, and paste it into the CLI. All test cases will automatically be logged (find more information on data privacy [here](https://deepeval.com/docs/data-privacy?utm_source=GitHub)).","","## Write your first test case","","Create a test file:","","```bash","touch test_chatbot.py","```","","Open `test_chatbot.py` and write your first test case to run an **end-to-end** evaluation using DeepEval, which treats your LLM app as a black-box:","","```python","import pytest","from deepeval import assert_test","from deepeval.metrics import GEval","from deepeval.test_case import LLMTestCase, SingleTurnParams","","def test_case():","    correctness_metric = GEval(","        name=\"Correctness\",","        criteria=\"Determine if the 'actual output' is correct based on the 'expected output'.\",","        evaluation_params=[SingleTurnParams.ACTUAL_OUTPUT, SingleTurnParams.EXPECTED_OUTPUT],","        threshold=0.5","    )","    test_case = LLMTestCase(","        input=\"What if these shoes don't fit?\",","        # Replace this with the actual output from your LLM application","        actual_output=\"You have 30 days to get a full refund at no extra cost.\",","        expected_output=\"We offer a 30-day full refund at no extra costs.\",","        retrieval_context=[\"All customers are eligible for a 30 day full refund at no extra costs.\"]","    )","    assert_test(test_case, [correctness_metric])","```","","Set your `OPENAI_API_KEY` as an environment variable (you can also evaluate using your own custom model, for more details visit [this part of our docs](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-introduction#using-a-custom-llm?utm_source=GitHub)):","","```","export OPENAI_API_KEY=\"...\"","```","","And finally, run `test_chatbot.py` in the CLI:","","```","deepeval test run test_chatbot.py","```","","**Congratulations! Your test case should have passed ✅** Let's break down what happened.","","- The variable `input` mimics a user input, and `actual_output` is a placeholder for what your application's supposed to output based on this input.","- The variable `expected_output` represents the ideal answer for a given `input`, and [`GEval`](https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-llm-evals) is a research-backed metric provided by `deepeval` for you to evaluate your LLM outputs on any custom with human-like accuracy.","- In this example, the metric `criteria` is correctness of the `actual_output` based on the provided `expected_output`.","- All metric scores range from 0 - 1, which the `threshold=0.5` threshold ultimately determines if your test has passed or not.","","[Read our documentation](https://deepeval.com/docs/getting-started?utm_source=GitHub) for more information!","","\u003cbr /\u003e","","## Evals With Full Traceability","","Use `evals_iterator()` to run the same dataset through your app, whether you instrument it manually or through one of DeepEval's framework integrations.","","Here's an example of manual instrumentation:","","```python","from deepeval.tracing import observe, update_current_span","from deepeval.test_case import LLMTestCase","from deepeval.metrics import TaskCompletionMetric","","@observe()","def inner_component(input: str):","    output = \"result\"","    update_current_span(test_case=LLMTestCase(input=input, actual_output=output))","    return output","","@observe()","def app(input: str):","    return inner_component(input)","","# This metric will be run on your trace end to end.","for golden in dataset.evals_iterator(metrics=[TaskCompletionMetric()]):","    app(golden.input)","```","","\u003cdetails\u003e","\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eOpenAI\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e","","```python","from deepeval.openai import OpenAI","from deepeval.tracing import trace","from deepeval.metrics import TaskCompletionMetric","","client = OpenAI()","","# This metric will be run on your trace end to end.","for golden in dataset.evals_iterator():","    with trace(metrics=[TaskCompletionMetric()]):","        client.chat.completions.create(","            model=\"gpt-4o\",","            messages=[{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": golden.input}],","        )","```","","\u003c/details\u003e","","\u003cdetails\u003e","\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eOpenAI Agents\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e","","```python","from agents import Runner","from deepeval.metrics import TaskCompletionMetric","","# This metric will be run on your trace end to end.","for golden in dataset.evals_iterator(metrics=[TaskCompletionMetric()]):","    Runner.run_sync(agent, golden.input)","```","","\u003c/details\u003e","","\u003cdetails\u003e","\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eAnthropic\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e","","```python","from deepeval.anthropic import Anthropic","from deepeval.tracing import trace","from deepeval.metrics import TaskCompletionMetric","","client = Anthropic()","","# This metric will be run on your trace end to end.","for golden in dataset.evals_iterator():","    with trace(metrics=[TaskCompletionMetric()]):","        client.messages.create(","            model=\"claude-sonnet-4-5\",","            max_tokens=1024,","            messages=[{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": golden.input}],","        )","```","","\u003c/details\u003e","","\u003cdetails\u003e","\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eLangChain\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e","","```python","from deepeval.integrations.langchain import CallbackHandler","from deepeval.metrics import TaskCompletionMetric","","# This metric will be run on your trace end to end.","for golden in dataset.evals_iterator():","    llm.invoke(","        golden.input,","        config={\"callbacks\": [CallbackHandler(metrics=[TaskCompletionMetric()])]},","    )","```","","\u003c/details\u003e","","\u003cdetails\u003e","\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eLangGraph\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e","","```python","from deepeval.integrations.langchain import CallbackHandler","from deepeval.metrics import TaskCompletionMetric","","# This metric will be run on your trace end to end.","for golden in dataset.evals_iterator():","    agent.invoke(","        {\"messages\": [{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": golden.input}]},","        config={\"callbacks\": [CallbackHandler(metrics=[TaskCompletionMetric()])]},","    )","```","","\u003c/details\u003e","","\u003cdetails\u003e","\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003ePydantic AI\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e","","```python","from deepeval.metrics import TaskCompletionMetric","","# This metric will be run on your trace end to end.","for golden in dataset.evals_iterator(metrics=[TaskCompletionMetric()]):","    agent.run_sync(golden.input)","```","","\u003c/details\u003e","","\u003cdetails\u003e","\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eCrewAI\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e","","```python","from deepeval.integrations.crewai import instrument_crewai","from deepeval.metrics import TaskCompletionMetric","","instrument_crewai()","","# This metric will be run on your trace end to end.","for golden in dataset.evals_iterator(metrics=[TaskCompletionMetric()]):","    crew.kickoff({\"input\": golden.input})","```","","\u003c/details\u003e","","\u003cdetails\u003e","\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eAWS AgentCore\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e","","```python","from deepeval.integrations.agentcore import instrument_agentcore","from deepeval.metrics import TaskCompletionMetric","","instrument_agentcore()","","# This metric will be run on your trace end to end.","for golden in dataset.evals_iterator(metrics=[TaskCompletionMetric()]):","    invoke({\"prompt\": golden.input})","```","","\u003c/details\u003e","","\u003cdetails\u003e","\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eLlamaIndex\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e","","```python","import asyncio","from deepeval.evaluate.configs import AsyncConfig","from deepeval.metrics import TaskCompletionMetric","","# This metric will be run on your trace end to end.","for golden in dataset.evals_iterator(","    async_config=AsyncConfig(run_async=True),","    metrics=[TaskCompletionMetric()],","):","    task = asyncio.create_task(agent.run(golden.input))","    dataset.evaluate(task)","```","","\u003c/details\u003e","","\u003cdetails\u003e","\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eGoogle ADK\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e","","```python","import asyncio","from deepeval.evaluate.configs import AsyncConfig","from deepeval.integrations.google_adk import instrument_google_adk","from deepeval.metrics import TaskCompletionMetric","","instrument_google_adk()","","# This metric will be run on your trace end to end.","for golden in dataset.evals_iterator(","    async_config=AsyncConfig(run_async=True),","    metrics=[TaskCompletionMetric()],","):","    task = asyncio.create_task(run_agent(golden.input))","    dataset.evaluate(task)","```","","\u003c/details\u003e","","\u003cdetails\u003e","\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eStrands\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e","","```python","from deepeval.integrations.strands import instrument_strands","from deepeval.metrics import TaskCompletionMetric","","instrument_strands()","","# This metric will be run on your trace end to end.","for golden in dataset.evals_iterator(metrics=[TaskCompletionMetric()]):","    agent(golden.input)","```","","\u003c/details\u003e","","Learn more about component-level evaluations [here.](https://www.deepeval.com/docs/evaluation-component-level-llm-evals)","","\u003cbr /\u003e","","## Evaluate Without Pytest Integration","","Alternatively, you can evaluate without Pytest, which is more suited for a notebook environment.","","```python","from deepeval import evaluate","from deepeval.metrics import AnswerRelevancyMetric","from deepeval.test_case import LLMTestCase","","answer_relevancy_metric = AnswerRelevancyMetric(threshold=0.7)","test_case = LLMTestCase(","    input=\"What if these shoes don't fit?\",","    # Replace this with the actual output from your LLM application","    actual_output=\"We offer a 30-day full refund at no extra costs.\",","    retrieval_context=[\"All customers are eligible for a 30 day full refund at no extra costs.\"]",")","evaluate([test_case], [answer_relevancy_metric])","```","","## Using Standalone Metrics","","DeepEval is extremely modular, making it easy for anyone to use any of our metrics. Continuing from the previous example:","","```python","from deepeval.metrics import AnswerRelevancyMetric","from deepeval.test_case import LLMTestCase","","answer_relevancy_metric = AnswerRelevancyMetric(threshold=0.7)","test_case = LLMTestCase(","    input=\"What if these shoes don't fit?\",","    # Replace this with the actual output from your LLM application","    actual_output=\"We offer a 30-day full refund at no extra costs.\",","    retrieval_context=[\"All customers are eligible for a 30 day full refund at no extra costs.\"]",")","","answer_relevancy_metric.measure(test_case)","print(answer_relevancy_metric.score)","# All metrics also offer an explanation","print(answer_relevancy_metric.reason)","```","","Note that some metrics are for RAG pipelines, while others are for fine-tuning. Make sure to use our docs to pick the right one for your use case.","","## A Note on Env Variables (.env / .env.local)","","DeepEval auto-loads `.env.local` then `.env` from the current working directory **at import time**.","**Precedence:** process env -\u003e `.env.local` -\u003e `.env`.","Opt out with `DEEPEVAL_DISABLE_DOTENV=1`.","","```bash","cp .env.example .env.local","# then edit .env.local (ignored by git)","```","","# DeepEval With Confident AI","","[Confident AI](https://www.confident-ai.com?utm_source=deepeval\u0026utm_medium=github\u0026utm_content=cli_login_section) is an all-in-one platform to manage datasets, trace LLM applications, and run evaluations in production. <p dir="auto"><strong>DeepEval</strong> is a simple-to-use, open-source LLM evaluation framework, for evaluating large-language model systems. It is similar to Pytest but specialized for unit testing LLM apps. DeepEval incorporates the latest research to run evals via metrics such as G-Eval, task completion, answer relevancy, hallucination, etc., which uses LLM-as-a-judge and other NLP models that run <strong>locally on your machine</strong>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Whether you're building AI agents, RAG pipelines, or chatbots, implemented via LangChain or OpenAI, DeepEval has you covered. With it, you can easily determine the optimal models, prompts, and architecture to improve your AI quality, prevent prompt drifting, or even transition from OpenAI to Claude with confidence.</p>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-important" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-report mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M0 1.75C0 .784.784 0 1.75 0h12.5C15.216 0 16 .784 16 1.75v9.5A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 14.25 13H8.06l-2.573 2.573A1.458 1.458 0 0 1 3 14.543V13H1.75A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 0 11.25Zm1.75-.25a.25.25 0 0 0-.25.25v9.5c0 .138.112.25.25.25h2a.75.75 0 0 1 .75.75v2.19l2.72-2.72a.749.749 0 0 1 .53-.22h6.5a.25.25 0 0 0 .25-.25v-9.5a.25.25 0 0 0-.25-.25Zm7 2.25v2.5a.75.75 0 0 1-1.5 0v-2.5a.75.75 0 0 1 1.5 0ZM9 9a1 1 0 1 1-2 0 1 1 0 0 1 2 0Z"></path></svg>Important</p><p dir="auto">Need a place for your DeepEval testing data to live 🏡❤️? <a href="https://www.confident-ai.com?utm_source=deepeval&amp;utm_medium=github&amp;utm_content=signup_callout" rel="nofollow">Sign up to Confident AI</a> to compare iterations of your LLM app, generate &amp; share testing reports, and more.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🔥 Metrics and Features</h1><a id="user-content--metrics-and-features" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🔥 Metrics and Features" href="#-metrics-and-features"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto">📐 Large variety of ready-to-use LLM eval metrics (all with explanations) powered by <strong>ANY</strong> LLM of your choice, statistical methods, or NLP models that run <strong>locally on your machine</strong> covering all use cases:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Custom, All-Purpose Metrics:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-llm-evals" rel="nofollow">G-Eval</a> — a research-backed LLM-as-a-judge metric for evaluating on any custom criteria with human-like accuracy</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-dag" rel="nofollow">DAG</a> — DeepEval's graph-based deterministic LLM-as-a-judge metric builder</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<details>
<summary><b>Agentic Metrics</b></summary>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-task-completion" rel="nofollow">Task Completion</a> — evaluate whether an agent accomplished its goal</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-tool-correctness" rel="nofollow">Tool Correctness</a> — check if the right tools were called with the right arguments</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-goal-accuracy" rel="nofollow">Goal Accuracy</a> — measure how accurately the agent achieved the intended goal</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-step-efficiency" rel="nofollow">Step Efficiency</a> — evaluate whether the agent took unnecessary steps</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-plan-adherence" rel="nofollow">Plan Adherence</a> — check if the agent followed the expected plan</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-plan-quality" rel="nofollow">Plan Quality</a> — evaluate the quality of the agent's plan</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-tool-use" rel="nofollow">Tool Use</a> — measure quality of tool usage</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-argument-correctness" rel="nofollow">Argument Correctness</a> — validate tool call arguments</li>
</ul>
</details>
</li>
<li>
<details>
<summary><b>RAG Metrics</b></summary>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-answer-relevancy" rel="nofollow">Answer Relevancy</a> — measure how relevant the RAG pipeline's output is to the input</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-faithfulness" rel="nofollow">Faithfulness</a> — evaluate whether the RAG pipeline's output factually aligns with the retrieval context</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-contextual-recall" rel="nofollow">Contextual Recall</a> — measure how well the RAG pipeline's retrieval context aligns with the expected output</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-contextual-precision" rel="nofollow">Contextual Precision</a> — evaluate whether relevant nodes in the RAG pipeline's retrieval context are ranked higher</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-contextual-relevancy" rel="nofollow">Contextual Relevancy</a> — measure the overall relevance of the RAG pipeline's retrieval context to the input</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-ragas" rel="nofollow">RAGAS</a> — average of answer relevancy, faithfulness, contextual precision, and contextual recall</li>
</ul>
</details>
</li>
<li>
<details>
<summary><b>Multi-Turn Metrics</b></summary>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-knowledge-retention" rel="nofollow">Knowledge Retention</a> — evaluate whether the chatbot retains factual information throughout a conversation</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-conversation-completeness" rel="nofollow">Conversation Completeness</a> — measure whether the chatbot satisfies user needs throughout a conversation</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-turn-relevancy" rel="nofollow">Turn Relevancy</a> — evaluate whether the chatbot generates consistently relevant responses throughout a conversation</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-turn-faithfulness" rel="nofollow">Turn Faithfulness</a> — check if the chatbot's responses are factually grounded in retrieval context across turns</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-role-adherence" rel="nofollow">Role Adherence</a> — evaluate whether the chatbot adheres to its assigned role throughout a conversation</li>
</ul>
</details>
</li>
<li>
<details>
<summary><b>MCP Metrics</b></summary>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-mcp-task-completion" rel="nofollow">MCP Task Completion</a> — evaluate how effectively an MCP-based agent accomplishes a task</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-mcp-use" rel="nofollow">MCP Use</a> — measure how effectively an agent uses its available MCP servers</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-multi-turn-mcp-use" rel="nofollow">Multi-Turn MCP Use</a> — evaluate MCP server usage across conversation turns</li>
</ul>
</details>
</li>
<li>
<details>
<summary><b>Multimodal Metrics</b></summary>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/multimodal-metrics-text-to-image" rel="nofollow">Text to Image</a> — evaluate image generation quality based on semantic consistency and perceptual quality</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/multimodal-metrics-image-editing" rel="nofollow">Image Editing</a> — evaluate image editing quality based on semantic consistency and perceptual quality</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/multimodal-metrics-image-coherence" rel="nofollow">Image Coherence</a> — measure how well images align with their accompanying text</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/multimodal-metrics-image-helpfulness" rel="nofollow">Image Helpfulness</a> — evaluate how effectively images contribute to user comprehension of the text</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/multimodal-metrics-image-reference" rel="nofollow">Image Reference</a> — evaluate how accurately images are referred to or explained by accompanying text</li>
</ul>
</details>
</li>
<li>
<details>
<summary><b>Other Metrics</b></summary>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-hallucination" rel="nofollow">Hallucination</a> — check whether the LLM generates factually correct information against provided context</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-summarization" rel="nofollow">Summarization</a> — evaluate whether summaries are factually correct and include necessary details</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-bias" rel="nofollow">Bias</a> — detect gender, racial, or political bias in LLM outputs</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-toxicity" rel="nofollow">Toxicity</a> — evaluate toxicity in LLM outputs</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-json-correctness" rel="nofollow">JSON Correctness</a> — check whether the output matches an expected JSON schema</li>
<li><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-prompt-alignment" rel="nofollow">Prompt Alignment</a> — measure whether the output aligns with instructions in the prompt template</li>
</ul>
</details>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">🎯 Supports both end-to-end and component-level LLM evaluation.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">🧩 Build your own custom metrics that are automatically integrated with DeepEval's ecosystem.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">🔮 Generate both single and multi-turn synthetic datasets for evaluation.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">🔗 Integrates seamlessly with <strong>ANY</strong> CI/CD environment.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">🧬 Optimize prompts automatically based on evaluation results.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">🏆 Easily benchmark <strong>ANY</strong> LLM on popular LLM benchmarks in <a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/benchmarks-introduction?utm_source=GitHub" rel="nofollow">under 10 lines of code.</a>, including MMLU, HellaSwag, DROP, BIG-Bench Hard, TruthfulQA, HumanEval, GSM8K.</p>
</li>
</ul>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🔌 Integrations</h1><a id="user-content--integrations" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🔌 Integrations" href="#-integrations"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">DeepEval plugs into any LLM framework — OpenAI Agents, LangChain, CrewAI, and more. To scale evals across your team — or let anyone run them without writing code — <strong>Confident AI</strong> gives you a native platform integration.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Frameworks</h2><a id="user-content-frameworks" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Frameworks" href="#frameworks"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">

</ul>

<p dir="auto"><a href="https://www.confident-ai.com?utm_source=deepeval&amp;utm_medium=github&amp;utm_content=platform_section" rel="nofollow">Confident AI</a> is an all-in-one platform that integrates natively with DeepEval.</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Manage datasets, trace LLM applications, run evaluations, and monitor responses in production — all from one platform.</li>
<li>Don't need a UI? Confident AI can also be your data persistent layer - run evals, pull datasets, and inspect traces straight from claude code, cursor, via Confident AI's <a href="https://github.com/confident-ai/confident-mcp-server">MCP server</a>.</li>
</ul>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🤖 Vibe-Coder QuickStart</h1><a id="user-content--vibe-coder-quickstart" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🤖 Vibe-Coder QuickStart" href="#-vibe-coder-quickstart"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Want your coding agent to add evals and fix failures for you? Install the DeepEval skill, point it at your agent, RAG pipeline, or chatbot, and ask it to generate a dataset, write the eval suite, run <code>deepeval test run</code>, and iterate on the failing metrics.</p>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/vibe-coder-quickstart?utm_source=GitHub" rel="nofollow">Start with the 5-minute vibe-coder guide</a>.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🚀 Human QuickStart</h1><a id="user-content--human-quickstart" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🚀 Human QuickStart" href="#-human-quickstart"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Let's pretend your LLM application is a RAG based customer support chatbot; here's how DeepEval can help test what you've built.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Installation</h2><a id="user-content-installation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Installation" href="#installation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Deepeval works with <strong>Python&gt;=3.9+</strong>.</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="pip install -U deepeval"><pre class="notranslate"><code>pip install -U deepeval
</code></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Create an account (highly recommended)</h2><a id="user-content-create-an-account-highly-recommended" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Create an account (highly recommended)" href="#create-an-account-highly-recommended"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Using the <code>deepeval</code> platform will allow you to generate sharable testing reports on the cloud. It is free, takes no additional code to setup, and we highly recommend giving it a try.</p>
<p dir="auto">To login, run:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="deepeval login"><pre class="notranslate"><code>deepeval login
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Follow the instructions in the CLI to create an account, copy your API key, and paste it into the CLI. All test cases will automatically be logged (find more information on data privacy <a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/data-privacy?utm_source=GitHub" rel="nofollow">here</a>).</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Write your first test case</h2><a id="user-content-write-your-first-test-case" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Write your first test case" href="#write-your-first-test-case"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Create a test file:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="touch test_chatbot.py"><pre>touch test_chatbot.py</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Open <code>test_chatbot.py</code> and write your first test case to run an <strong>end-to-end</strong> evaluation using DeepEval, which treats your LLM app as a black-box:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import pytest
from deepeval import assert_test
from deepeval.metrics import GEval
from deepeval.test_case import LLMTestCase, SingleTurnParams

def test_case():
    correctness_metric = GEval(
        name=&quot;Correctness&quot;,
        criteria=&quot;Determine if the 'actual output' is correct based on the 'expected output'.&quot;,
        evaluation_params=[SingleTurnParams.ACTUAL_OUTPUT, SingleTurnParams.EXPECTED_OUTPUT],
        threshold=0.5
    )
    test_case = LLMTestCase(
        input=&quot;What if these shoes don't fit?&quot;,
        # Replace this with the actual output from your LLM application
        actual_output=&quot;You have 30 days to get a full refund at no extra cost.&quot;,
        expected_output=&quot;We offer a 30-day full refund at no extra costs.&quot;,
        retrieval_context=[&quot;All customers are eligible for a 30 day full refund at no extra costs.&quot;]
    )
    assert_test(test_case, [correctness_metric])"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">pytest</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">deepeval</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">assert_test</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">deepeval</span>.<span class="pl-s1">metrics</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">GEval</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">deepeval</span>.<span class="pl-s1">test_case</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">LLMTestCase</span>, <span class="pl-v">SingleTurnParams</span>

<span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">test_case</span>():
    <span class="pl-s1">correctness_metric</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">GEval</span>(
        <span class="pl-s1">name</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"Correctness"</span>,
        <span class="pl-s1">criteria</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"Determine if the 'actual output' is correct based on the 'expected output'."</span>,
        <span class="pl-s1">evaluation_params</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>[<span class="pl-v">SingleTurnParams</span>.<span class="pl-c1">ACTUAL_OUTPUT</span>, <span class="pl-v">SingleTurnParams</span>.<span class="pl-c1">EXPECTED_OUTPUT</span>],
        <span class="pl-s1">threshold</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0.5</span>
    )
    <span class="pl-s1">test_case</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">LLMTestCase</span>(
        <span class="pl-s1">input</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"What if these shoes don't fit?"</span>,
        <span class="pl-c"># Replace this with the actual output from your LLM application</span>
        <span class="pl-s1">actual_output</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"You have 30 days to get a full refund at no extra cost."</span>,
        <span class="pl-s1">expected_output</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"We offer a 30-day full refund at no extra costs."</span>,
        <span class="pl-s1">retrieval_context</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>[<span class="pl-s">"All customers are eligible for a 30 day full refund at no extra costs."</span>]
    )
    <span class="pl-en">assert_test</span>(<span class="pl-s1">test_case</span>, [<span class="pl-s1">correctness_metric</span>])</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Set your <code>OPENAI_API_KEY</code> as an environment variable (you can also evaluate using your own custom model, for more details visit <a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-introduction#using-a-custom-llm?utm_source=GitHub" rel="nofollow">this part of our docs</a>):</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="export OPENAI_API_KEY=&quot;...&quot;"><pre class="notranslate"><code>export OPENAI_API_KEY="..."
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">And finally, run <code>test_chatbot.py</code> in the CLI:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="deepeval test run test_chatbot.py"><pre class="notranslate"><code>deepeval test run test_chatbot.py
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Congratulations! Your test case should have passed ✅</strong> Let's break down what happened.</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>The variable <code>input</code> mimics a user input, and <code>actual_output</code> is a placeholder for what your application's supposed to output based on this input.</li>
<li>The variable <code>expected_output</code> represents the ideal answer for a given <code>input</code>, and <a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/metrics-llm-evals" rel="nofollow"><code>GEval</code></a> is a research-backed metric provided by <code>deepeval</code> for you to evaluate your LLM outputs on any custom with human-like accuracy.</li>
<li>In this example, the metric <code>criteria</code> is correctness of the <code>actual_output</code> based on the provided <code>expected_output</code>.</li>
<li>All metric scores range from 0 - 1, which the <code>threshold=0.5</code> threshold ultimately determines if your test has passed or not.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://deepeval.com/docs/getting-started?utm_source=GitHub" rel="nofollow">Read our documentation</a> for more information!</p>
<br>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Evals With Full Traceability</h2><a id="user-content-evals-with-full-traceability" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Evals With Full Traceability" href="#evals-with-full-traceability"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Use <code>evals_iterator()</code> to run the same dataset through your app, whether you instrument it manually or through one of DeepEval's framework integrations.</p>
<p dir="auto">Here's an example of manual instrumentation:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from deepeval.tracing import observe, update_current_span
from deepeval.test_case import LLMTestCase
from deepeval.metrics import TaskCompletionMetric

@observe()
def inner_component(input: str):
    output = &quot;result&quot;
    update_current_span(test_case=LLMTestCase(input=input, actual_output=output))
    return output

@observe()
def app(input: str):
    return inner_component(input)

# This metric will be run on your trace end to end.
for golden in dataset.evals_iterator(metrics=[TaskCompletionMetric()]):
    app(golden.input)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">deepeval</span>.<span class="pl-s1">tracing</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">observe</span>, <span class="pl-s1">update_current_span</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">deepeval</span>.<span class="pl-s1">test_case</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">LLMTestCase</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">deepeval</span>.<span class="pl-s1">metrics</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">TaskCompletionMetric</span>

<span class="pl-en">@<span class="pl-en">observe</span>()</span>
<span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">inner_component</span>(<span class="pl-s1">input</span>: <span class="pl-smi">str</span>):
    <span class="pl-s1">output</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">"result"</span>
    <span class="pl-en">update_current_span</span>(<span class="pl-s1">test_case</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-en">LLMTestCase</span>(<span class="pl-s1">input</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">input</span>, <span class="pl-s1">actual_output</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">output</span>))
    <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-s1">output</span>

<span class="pl-en">@<span class="pl-en">observe</span>()</span>
<span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">app</span>(<span class="pl-s1">input</span>: <span class="pl-smi">str</span>):
    <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-en">inner_component</span>(<span class="pl-s1">input</span>)

<span class="pl-c"># This metric will be run on your trace end to end.</span>
<span class="pl-k">for</span> <span class="pl-s1">golden</span> <span class="pl-c1">in</span> <span class="pl-s1">dataset</span>.<span class="pl-c1">evals_iterator</span>(<span class="pl-s1">metrics</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>[<span class="pl-en">TaskCompletionMetric</span>()]):
    <span class="pl-en">app</span>(<span class="pl-s1">golden</span>.<span class="pl-c1">input</span>)</pre></div>

<span class="pl-c"># This metric will be run on your trace end to end.</span>
<span class="pl-k">for</span> <span class="pl-s1">golden</span> <span class="pl-c1">in</span> <span class="pl-s1">dataset</span>.<span class="pl-c1">evals_iterator</span>(<span class="pl-s1">metrics</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>[<span class="pl-en">TaskCompletionMetric</span>()]):
    <span class="pl-s1">crew</span>.<span class="pl-c1">kickoff</span>({<span class="pl-s">"input"</span>: <span class="pl-s1">golden</span>.<span class="pl-c1">input</span>})</pre></div>
</details>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Evaluate Without Pytest Integration</h2><a id="user-content-evaluate-without-pytest-integration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Evaluate Without Pytest Integration" href="#evaluate-without-pytest-integration"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Alternatively, you can evaluate without Pytest, which is more suited for a notebook environment.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from deepeval import evaluate
from deepeval.metrics import AnswerRelevancyMetric
from deepeval.test_case import LLMTestCase

answer_relevancy_metric = AnswerRelevancyMetric(threshold=0.7)
test_case = LLMTestCase(
    input=&quot;What if these shoes don't fit?&quot;,
    # Replace this with the actual output from your LLM application
    actual_output=&quot;We offer a 30-day full refund at no extra costs.&quot;,
    retrieval_context=[&quot;All customers are eligible for a 30 day full refund at no extra costs.&quot;]
)
evaluate([test_case], [answer_relevancy_metric])"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">deepeval</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">evaluate</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">deepeval</span>.<span class="pl-s1">metrics</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">AnswerRelevancyMetric</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">deepeval</span>.<span class="pl-s1">test_case</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">LLMTestCase</span>

<span class="pl-s1">answer_relevancy_metric</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">AnswerRelevancyMetric</span>(<span class="pl-s1">threshold</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0.7</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">test_case</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">LLMTestCase</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">input</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"What if these shoes don't fit?"</span>,
    <span class="pl-c"># Replace this with the actual output from your LLM application</span>
    <span class="pl-s1">actual_output</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"We offer a 30-day full refund at no extra costs."</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">retrieval_context</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>[<span class="pl-s">"All customers are eligible for a 30 day full refund at no extra costs."</span>]
)
<span class="pl-en">evaluate</span>([<span class="pl-s1">test_case</span>], [<span class="pl-s1">answer_relevancy_metric</span>])</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Using Standalone Metrics</h2><a id="user-content-using-standalone-metrics" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Using Standalone Metrics" href="#using-standalone-metrics"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">DeepEval is extremely modular, making it easy for anyone to use any of our metrics. Continuing from the previous example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from deepeval.metrics import AnswerRelevancyMetric
from deepeval.test_case import LLMTestCase

answer_relevancy_metric = AnswerRelevancyMetric(threshold=0.7)
test_case = LLMTestCase(
    input=&quot;What if these shoes don't fit?&quot;,
    # Replace this with the actual output from your LLM application
    actual_output=&quot;We offer a 30-day full refund at no extra costs.&quot;,
    retrieval_context=[&quot;All customers are eligible for a 30 day full refund at no extra costs.&quot;]
)

answer_relevancy_metric.measure(test_case)
print(answer_relevancy_metric.score)
# All metrics also offer an explanation
print(answer_relevancy_metric.reason)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">deepeval</span>.<span class="pl-s1">metrics</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">AnswerRelevancyMetric</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">deepeval</span>.<span class="pl-s1">test_case</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">LLMTestCase</span>

<span class="pl-s1">answer_relevancy_metric</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">AnswerRelevancyMetric</span>(<span class="pl-s1">threshold</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0.7</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">test_case</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">LLMTestCase</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">input</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"What if these shoes don't fit?"</span>,
    <span class="pl-c"># Replace this with the actual output from your LLM application</span>
    <span class="pl-s1">actual_output</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"We offer a 30-day full refund at no extra costs."</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">retrieval_context</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>[<span class="pl-s">"All customers are eligible for a 30 day full refund at no extra costs."</span>]
)

<span class="pl-s1">answer_relevancy_metric</span>.<span class="pl-c1">measure</span>(<span class="pl-s1">test_case</span>)
<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">answer_relevancy_metric</span>.<span class="pl-c1">score</span>)
<span class="pl-c"># All metrics also offer an explanation</span>
<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">answer_relevancy_metric</span>.<span class="pl-c1">reason</span>)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Note that some metrics are for RAG pipelines, while others are for fine-tuning. Make sure to use our docs to pick the right one for your use case.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">A Note on Env Variables (.env / .env.local)</h2><a id="user-content-a-note-on-env-variables-env--envlocal" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: A Note on Env Variables (.env / .env.local)" href="#a-note-on-env-variables-env--envlocal"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">DeepEval auto-loads <code>.env.local</code> then <code>.env</code> from the current working directory <strong>at import time</strong>.
<strong>Precedence:</strong> process env -&gt; <code>.env.local</code> -&gt; <code>.env</code>.
Opt out with <code>DEEPEVAL_DISABLE_DOTENV=1</code>.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="cp .env.example .env.local
# then edit .env.local (ignored by git)"><pre>cp .env.example .env.local
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> then edit .env.local (ignored by git)</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">DeepEval With Confident AI</h1><a id="user-content-deepeval-with-confident-ai" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: DeepEval With Confident AI" href="#deepeval-with-confident-ai"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://www.confident-ai.com?utm_source=deepeval&amp;utm_medium=github&amp;utm_content=cli_login_section" rel="nofollow">Confident AI</a> is an all-in-one platform to manage datasets, trace LLM applications, and run evaluations in production. Log in from the CLI to get started:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="deepeval login"><pre>deepeval login</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Then run your tests as usual — results are automatically synced to the platform:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="deepeval test run test_chatbot.py"><pre>deepeval <span class="pl-c1">test</span> run test_chatbot.py</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="/confident-ai/deepeval/blob/main/assets/demo.gif"><img src="/confident-ai/deepeval/raw/main/assets/demo.gif" alt="Demo GIF" data-animated-image="" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<p dir="auto">Prefer to stay in your IDE? Use DeepEval via <a href="https://github.com/confident-ai/confident-mcp-server">Confident AI's MCP server</a> as the persistent layer to run evals, pull datasets, and inspect traces without leaving your editor.</p>
<p align="center" dir="auto">
  <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="/confident-ai/deepeval/blob/main/assets/confident-mcp-architecture.png"><img src="/confident-ai/deepeval/raw/main/assets/confident-mcp-architecture.png" alt="Confident AI MCP Architecture" width="500" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
</p>
<p dir="auto">Everything on Confident AI is available <a href="https://www.confident-ai.com/docs?utm_source=deepeval&amp;utm_medium=github&amp;utm_content=cloud_docs" rel="nofollow">here</a>.</p>
